That\u0027s not universally true\u2014plenty of modern designs incorporate pockets\u2014but many do not. Designers might assume that women carry purses or that they\u0027d prefer form-flattering clothes over functional options.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;We [women] know clearly we need pockets to carry technology and I think it\u0027s expected we are going to carry a purse,\u0026quot; Camilla Olson, creative director of a fashion firm that shares her name, \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.theatlantic.com\/technology\/archive\/2014\/09\/the-gender-politics-of-pockets\/380935\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Etold\u003C\/a\u003E \u003Cem\u003EThe Atlantic\u003C\/em\u003E. \u0026quot;When we\u0027re working we don\u2019t carry purses around. A pocket is a reasonable thing.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EOlson went on to say that the fashion industry is keeping women \u0026quot;in a certain place\u0026quot; and preventing them from moving forward professionally by avoiding pockets.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EThat might sound like an overreaction, but it seems more reasonable in the context of the complicated history of the pocket. Prior to the 17th century, most people carried essential belongings with them, and pockets started as small bags that hung from the belt. \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.vox.com\/2016\/9\/19\/12865560\/politics-of-pockets-suffragettes-women\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003ESome time\u003C\/a\u003E in the 1600s, the bags were sewn onto the inside of men\u0027s pants, and women started to wear their tied-on bags under their dresses, accessing them through slits in their clothing. That changed in the late 1700s and early 1800s when dress styles became too slim to accommodate the bags.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;The design of the times was \u0027Greek Goddesses.\u0027 Women ... would study the ancient texts and couldn\u0027t find pockets, so they didn\u0027t use them in the dress,\u0026quot; Elizabeth Morano, a professor at Parsons School of Design, said of this era of fashion in an interview with Marketplace. \u0026quot;Some of those stories are just stories, but the line was a lot more sleek. Think of the neoclassical dress. It\u0027s straight up and down. The line of the clothing changes completely.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EBy the 19th century, some women had started pushing for equality in fashion. In an Aug. 28, 1899 piece in \u003Cem\u003EThe New York Times\u003C\/em\u003E, a tailor noted that some women asked for pockets in their bicycle \u0026quot;costumes\u0026quot; to hold revolvers.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;Not all of them want to carry a revolver,\u0026quot; the tailor \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/timesmachine.nytimes.com\/timesmachine\/1899\/08\/28\/102534376.html?pageNumber=7\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Esaid\u003C\/a\u003E, \u0026quot;but a large percentage do and make no \u0027bones\u0027 about saying so.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u003Cem\u003EThat New York Times\u003C\/em\u003E piece also quoted an anonymous \u0026quot;Philosopher of Small Things\u0026quot; with this bit of tongue-in-cheek wisdom:\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cblockquote\u003E\u0026quot;Assuming that Adam and Eve both began life without any pockets, it seems to me that the difference in the progress of the sexes toward pockets illustrates and proves the superiority of the male. Man\u0027s pockets have developed, improved, and increased with the advances of civilization. Woman is actually retrograding\u2014losing ground and pockets.\u0026quot;\u003C\/blockquote\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EOver a century later, feminine fashion has certainly changed significantly in positive ways, but women\u0027s pockets don\u2019t hold a candle (or anything else) to men\u0027s pockets. In 2018, research from \u003Cem\u003EThe Pudding\u003C\/em\u003E \u003Ca href=https:\/\/pudding.cool\/2018\/08\/pockets\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Efound that\u003C\/a\u003E, on average, the pockets in women\u0027s jeans are 48 percent shorter and 6.5 percent narrower than men\u0027s pockets.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EOf course, women are typically smaller than men, but the researchers measured jeans with the same 32-inch waistband. The jeans should have been made to fit the same size person, but pocket size still varied between the sexes.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ESome designers believe that adding pockets to women\u0027s clothes (along with other functional improvements) could actually promote gender equality. For that to happen, women would have to purchase the new designs\u2014and that could happen fairly soon, as \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.independent.co.uk\/life-style\/fashion\/wedding-dresses-pockets-trend-comfort-marriage-ceremony-bridal-fashion-a8522121.html\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Epocketed dresses\u003C\/a\u003E and pants are becoming more popular. Microwaves have electric and magnetic fields, and minerals within the vegetables \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.inspection.gc.ca\/food\/information-for-consumers\/fact-sheets-and-infographics\/products-and-risks\/fruits-and-vegetables\/sparks-when-cooking\/eng\/1332278105073\/1332278331477\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Eact as antennae\u003C\/a\u003E for the electric fields.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EWhen there\u2019s a difference between the electrical properties between two bits of material\u2014say, the surface of one piece of kale and the surface of another nearby piece\u2014it creates an arc of electricity, which we see as a spark. The electrical charge moves from the material with high electrical potential to a surface with a lower electrical potential. Metal utensils and compact discs spark up for the same reason, although with those materials, the phenomena is much more destructive (since there\u2019s a larger amount of conductive metal).\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ELet\u2019s get back to the electrified veggies. Are they safe to eat?\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EYes, but you shouldn\u2019t cook food this way. If the food in question is dry enough, it can catch on fire. This rarely happens with vegetables; in most cases, the food will simply crisp up, and the microwave might shut off automatically to protect its magnetron tube.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EVegetables are more likely to catch on fire if they\u2019re not smooth\u2014crinkly kale, for instance\u2014and salt might help to cause the reaction, as it changes the electric potential of the materials. In the next five years, NASA plans to launch a spaceship to stay in orbit around the moon, \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.nasa.gov\/topics\/moon-to-mars\/overview\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Eoffering opportunities\u003C\/a\u003E to \u0026quot;test new tools, instruments and equipment that could be used on Mars, including human habitats, life support systems, and technologies and practices that could help [them] build self-sustaining outposts away from Earth.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ENASA\u2019s goal is to land crewed missions on Mars in the 2030s.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;If we bring together the capabilities and resources of our international and commercial partners to take us forward to the Moon and on to Mars, we will demonstrate to people around the world the power of a unified purpose,\u0026quot; the administration \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.nasa.gov\/specials\/moon2mars\/index.html\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Ewrote\u003C\/a\u003E on its website. \u0026quot;It will serve as an unparalleled and inspiring example of what humanity can do when it comes together to achieve a common goal for the common good.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EMars Base Camp\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EA proposed component of the NASA plan, Mars Base Camp was developed by Lockheed Martin. It would consist of a space station orbiting Mars capable of supporting crews on 1,000-day missions. It could generate hydrogen and oxygen from water, and craft sent from Base Camp would be reusable.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;With these additional developments, the Mars Base Camp concept can be seen as a core system that pivots humanity into a viable, sustainable long-term Mars exploration program,\u0026quot; Lockheed Martin scientists wrote in a \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.lockheedmartin.com\/content\/dam\/lockheed-martin\/eo\/photo\/webt\/Mars-Base-Camp-Update-and-New-Concepts.pdf\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Etechnical paper\u003C\/a\u003E released by the company.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ESpaceX\u2019s Mars Missions\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EPrivate space exploration company SpaceX wants to beat NASA to Mars, and a first cargo mission is planned for 2022.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;The objectives for the first mission will be to confirm water resources, identify hazards, and put in place initial power, mining, and life support infrastructure,\u0026quot; SpaceX\u2019s \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.spacex.com\/mars\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Ewebsite reads\u003C\/a\u003E. \u0026quot;A second mission, with both cargo and crew, is targeted for 2024, with primary objectives of building a propellant depot and preparing for future crew flights. The ships from these initial missions will also serve as the beginnings of the first Mars base, from which we can build a thriving city and eventually a self-sustaining civilization on Mars.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EOver the last few decades, a number of private companies and government organizations have aborted missions to the planet.\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EFor example, in 2012, a Dutch company called Mars One envisioned a mission that would put the components of a base on Mars by 2023. However, that company \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/spacenews.com\/mars-one-company-goes-bankrupt\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Ewent bankrupt\u003C\/a\u003E in January 2019.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ESo, will we really land on Mars by 2024, or even 2030? It\u2019s possible, though unlikely. Before humanity heads to Mars, we\u2019ll need to figure out an incredible number of technical issues, and that\u2019s not cheap\u2014NASA plans on \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/qz.com\/1273644\/heres-how-nasa-and-spacex-chart-the-price-of-a-trip-to-mars\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Ecapping costs\u003C\/a\u003E at $220 billion. However, engineers at both SpaceX and NASA insist that the mission will eventually happen, and they\u2019ve set aspirational deadlines for the goal in order to maintain public interest.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;To many engineers, students and others, if you tell someone that something is going to happen in twenty years, twenty years is never,\u0026quot; Josh Brost, a SpaceX executive, said at the 2018 Humans to Mars Summit. \u0026quot;Fifteen years is hard to be motivated. ","flag":0,"created_at":"2010-12-05 11:40:15","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-08-29 15:37:02","pivot":{"question_id":409265508,"topic_id":470,"id":100493232}},{"id":3273,"slug":"transportation-and-logistics","title":"Transportation and Logistics","description":"Logistics refers to the act of coordinating and managing the transfer of goods from a source to a specific destination. Transportation is a vital part of logistics as it requires the use of trucks or ships in moving the goods.","flag":0,"created_at":"2007-11-30 14:31:41","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-08-29 15:38:16","pivot":{"question_id":409265508,"topic_id":3273,"id":100493233}}],"answers":[{"id":36491880,"question_id":409265508,"user_id":12,"body":"\u003Cp\u003EIt\u2019s actually pretty complex. Traffic light patterns vary considerably from country to country, although traffic lights in the United States \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.nema.org\/Standards\/Pages\/Traffic-Control-Systems.aspx\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Etypically follow\u003C\/a\u003E the National Electrical Manufacturers Association TS-1 or TS-2 standards.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ESo, let\u2019s say you\u2019re an engineer, and your job is to set up patterns for a city\u2019s traffic lights. You want to keep vehicles moving wherever possible while allowing sufficient time for pedestrians to cross the street. It\u2019s an important job\u2014poorly timed traffic lights \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.eastbaytimes.com\/2005\/09\/24\/badly-timed-traffic-signals-cost-union-city-big-bucks\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Ecan lead\u003C\/a\u003E to inaccurate citations for drivers and hundreds of thousands of dollars in lost revenue for cities. How will you make sure you\u2019ve got the best possible system?\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EYou could simply go with a fixed time control. In this system, the signals cycle after a set number of seconds and cannot change the length of these cycles based on current traffic. At a small intersection, that might be sufficient, but at heavy-traffic intersections, more attention might be required.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EHere are some of the factors you might consider:\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cul\u003E\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ESpeed Limits and Clearance Intervals -\u003C\/strong\u003E The most obvious (and arguably the most important) factor is the speed of the cars moving past the signals. The faster the cars, the more of them can move through on the green cycle.\u003C\/br\u003E\n\u003Cbr\u003EWhen the light starts to change, it will take a few seconds for the intersection to clear. Traffic controllers need to know these \u0026quot;clearance intervals\u0026quot; when setting the signal cycle lengths\u2014obviously, you don\u2019t want cars entering the intersection while other vehicles are still there.\u003C\/li\u003E\u003C\/br\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EPhysical Characteristics of the Intersection -\u003C\/strong\u003E How large is the intersection? Is it on a grade? Is the road made of asphalt or another material?\u003C\/br\u003E\n\u003Cbr\u003ENo two intersections are alike, and traffic controllers need to consider every unique factor to optimize times properly.\u003C\/li\u003E\u003C\/br\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ETraffic Light Triggers -\u003C\/strong\u003E Intersections often need different cycle lengths during different times of day. After all, if most traffic moves through an intersection in one direction at noon but in different directions during rush hour, you can\u2019t rely on the same traffic signal cycles to keep vehicles moving. Controllers need to consider traffic patterns to work effectively.\u003C\/br\u003E\n\u003Cbr\u003EMany signals use triggers to determine when vehicles or pedestrians are in position. Traffic light patterns might be triggered by sensors, video cameras, or buttons that pedestrians press to indicate they want to cross (although in some cities, those buttons \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.cnn.com\/style\/article\/placebo-buttons-design\/index.html\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Emight not be hooked up\u003C\/a\u003E to anything at all).\u003C\/li\u003E\u003C\/ul\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ESo, how do you negotiate all of those factors when setting up your traffic light patterns? You don\u2019t\u2014you let a computer handle it. Most modern traffic signals utilize sophisticated algorithms, and computer software makes changes to the patterns to keep the signals running smoothly.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EOf course, engineers still need to develop those algorithms in the first place, and they\u2019ll look at the unique characteristics of each intersection to keep drivers moving. It\u2019s an extremely complicated science, and it\u2019s something that most drivers take for granted.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EThe next time you find yourself hitting a series of perfectly timed green lights on your morning commute, be sure to take a moment to think of the engineers (and computers) that made the experience possible.\u003C\/p\u003E","created_at":"2019-10-07 14:36:31","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-10-15 19:18:51","vote_count":{"vote_count":2},"user":{"name":"Kevin Stringer","avatar":null,"id":12,"tagline":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - James Brown","meta":[{"user_id":12,"key":"tagline","value":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - James Brown"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optInEmail","value":"0"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optInNotifications","value":"1"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optOutAll","value":"0"}]}},{"id":409265507,"user_id":12,"slug":"What_happens_to_an_ant_when_its_separated_from_its_colony","title":"What happens to an ant when it\u0027s separated from its colony","body":null,"created_at":"2019-10-07 14:09:18","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-10-14 11:09:21","link":"https:\/\/www.answers.com\/Q\/What_happens_to_an_ant_when_its_separated_from_its_colony","topics":[{"id":2886,"slug":"animal-behavior","title":"Animal Behavior","description":"This category is for questions about an animal\u0027s visible muscular activity that constitutes behavior, as well as the proximate and ultimate causes.","flag":0,"created_at":"2009-08-21 20:08:29","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-08-29 15:38:03","pivot":{"question_id":409265507,"topic_id":2886,"id":100493228}},{"id":2885,"slug":"animal-life","title":"Animal Life","description":"Animals enrich our lives in so many different ways. The Animal Life category is for questions and answers about animals of all types, including pets, sea life, and farm animals. You can ask questions or find information regarding animals, their history, care, health issues, breeding, and behavior.","flag":0,"created_at":"2009-06-22 18:45:15","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-08-29 15:38:03","pivot":{"question_id":409265507,"topic_id":2885,"id":100493229}},{"id":2612,"slug":"ants","title":"Ants","description":"Ants are small social insects that live in colonies with queens. Varieties of ants include Carpenter, Leaf Cutter, Army, and Fire Ants, among others.","flag":0,"created_at":"2012-01-06 16:46:28","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-08-29 15:37:53","pivot":{"question_id":409265507,"topic_id":2612,"id":100493230}}],"answers":[{"id":36491879,"question_id":409265507,"user_id":12,"body":"\u003Cp\u003ETypically, it finds its way home. The mechanisms it uses to do that depend on the exact species of ant.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ESome rely on pheromone trails as their primary means of navigation; if these ants get lost, they\u2019ll wander around, looking for the trail of other ants.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ESome ants use visual landmarks when they\u2019re far from their colonies. In \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.livescience.com\/495-ants-navigate.html\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Eone study\u003C\/a\u003E, scientists led wood ants along a straight line next to a black wall to get a sugar reward. The wall would be on the left side of the ants as they walked toward the reward and on their right side when they walked back. The researchers then placed the ants midway along the wall; some of those ants had been fed, while others had not. The hungry ants kept the wall on their left, walking toward the sugar, while the ants that already ate walked back home.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EHere\u2019s the cool part: The researchers then placed the ants between two black walls. The hungry ants still walked closer to the right wall, and the well-fed ants walked closer to the left wall. That implies that the ants had formed memories, and that they were able to use those memories to navigate.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ESo, lost ants can use pheromones and visual cues. What about when they can\u2019t those things?\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EAgain, that depends on the species, but \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.nature.com\/articles\/s41598-017-14036-1\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Emany will use\u003C\/a\u003E a search pattern to try to find those all-helpful pheromones and landmarks, or other signals that could help them navigate home.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ELike many other insects, ants are capable of tracking distance and direction traveled, and they can calculate a vector pointing back to their homes. When they lose this vector, they start traveling in small loops, gradually expanding the loops until they\u2019re able to find helpful information.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EWe know that thanks to a 2017 study published in \u003Cem\u003EScientific Reports\u003C\/em\u003E, which compared the actions of experienced ants that had traveled a set route for several days with the actions of inexperienced ants (delightfully referred to as \u0026quot;na\u00efve ants\u0026quot; in the research).\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;At the unfamiliar test site, na\u00efve ants ran off a longer portion of their vector from path integration than did experienced ants,\u0026quot; the authors wrote. \u0026quot;Na\u00efve ants also spread out in their systematic search slower than did experienced ants. We conclude that as ants learn the views encountered on their familiar route better, they identify more readily unfamiliar views.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EIn other words, most ants don\u2019t rely on pheromone trails alone. They develop memories of their paths, then look for cues that will help them get home. Their search paths help them find those cues as quickly as possible.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EAll has failed. An ant can\u2019t find its way back. What does it do?\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EGenerally, they panic.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EAgain, the specific reaction depends on the species, but we do know that isolation is bad news for all types of ants.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EOne study found that when some ant species were placed alone in containers with adequate food and water, they died after six and a half days. They became hyperactive, searching for scent trails and visual cues that might lead them to their colonies\u2014even though they had access to food, they kept looking for ways to get home.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;I think it\u2019s somehow expected,\u0026quot; Akiko Koto, co-author of that study, \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.newyorker.com\/tech\/annals-of-technology\/lonely-ant\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Etold\u003C\/a\u003E \u003Cem\u003EThe New Yorker\u003C\/em\u003E. \u0026quot;In a natural condition, in the park or in a forest, if the ants lose their colonies, they\u2019ll try to find their mother colonies.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EIf ants can\u2019t find a way home, they eventually die from exhaustion or freeze to death. Like other insects, \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/f1000research.com\/articles\/2-280\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Eants are\u003C\/a\u003E \u003Cem\u003Eectothermic\u003C\/em\u003E, which means that they\u2019re dependent on external sources of body heat. Their colony provides that heat during cold nights, but if they can\u2019t make it back to their colonies, they\u2019re usually done for.\u003C\/p\u003E","created_at":"2019-10-07 14:23:36","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-10-07 14:24:06","vote_count":{"vote_count":2},"user":{"name":"Kevin Stringer","avatar":null,"id":12,"tagline":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - James Brown","meta":[{"user_id":12,"key":"tagline","value":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - James Brown"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optInEmail","value":"0"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optInNotifications","value":"1"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optOutAll","value":"0"}]}}],"vote_count":{"id":57824032,"vote_count":0,"votable_id":409265507,"votable_type":"App\\Models\\Question","created_at":"2019-10-07 14:39:24","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-10-14 11:09:21"},"meta":[{"id":9,"question_id":409265507,"key":"is_locked","value":"false","created_at":"2019-10-15 20:33:01","updated_at":"2019-10-15 20:41:56"}],"user":{"id":12,"username":"stringer","name":"Kevin Stringer","tagline":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - James Brown","meta":[{"user_id":12,"key":"tagline","value":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - James Brown"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optInEmail","value":"0"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optInNotifications","value":"1"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optOutAll","value":"0"}]}},{"id":409265505,"user_id":12,"slug":"Do_some_types_of_cars_get_more_traffic_tickets_than_others","title":"Do some types of cars get more traffic tickets than others","body":null,"created_at":"2019-10-03 15:12:44","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-10-07 22:09:24","link":"https:\/\/www.answers.com\/Q\/Do_some_types_of_cars_get_more_traffic_tickets_than_others","topics":[{"id":2123,"slug":"traffic-violations-and-tickets","title":"Traffic Violations and Tickets","description":"Drivers who exceed the speed limit or violate other traffic laws are subject to tickets. ","flag":0,"created_at":"2010-12-05 11:40:15","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-08-29 15:37:02","pivot":{"question_id":409265505,"topic_id":470,"id":100493223}},{"id":4399,"slug":"police-and-law-enforcement","title":"Police and Law Enforcement","description":"Agencies dedicated to preserving the obedience of criminal laws and order within a society","flag":0,"created_at":"2008-07-03 20:00:06","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-08-29 15:38:51","pivot":{"question_id":409265505,"topic_id":4399,"id":100493224}}],"answers":[{"id":36491877,"question_id":409265505,"user_id":12,"body":"\u003Cp\u003EIf you\u2019re hoping to avoid tickets, your best bet is to drive safely. With that said, yes, there\u2019s some evidence to indicate that vehicle choice does correlate with the likelihood of receiving a traffic ticket.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EFirst, to dispel an old myth, red cars don\u2019t receive a much greater proportional percentage of traffic tickets than cars of other colors. The misconception comes from the idea that drivers act more aggressively when driving red cars, or that police are more likely to notice red vehicles that commit infractions.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EThere\u2019s simply no evidence to support that claim. The \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.snopes.com\/fact-check\/red-handed\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Emost recent data\u003C\/a\u003E we have from that is from 1990, when a reporter for the \u003Cem\u003ESt. Petersburg Times\u003C\/em\u003E looked at 924 speeding citations in two counties. Red cars accounted for 14 percent of the total local vehicle population and about 16 percent of speeding citations.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EThat\u2019s within a reasonable margin of error, so if red cars get more tickets, the difference probably isn\u2019t substantial. That survey was limited, as it only observed two counties in Florida, but insurance statistics don\u2019t support the idea that red cars attract cops\u0027 attention.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;There is no data to support the assertion that red cars receive more traffic tickets than cars of any other color,\u0026quot; Carolyn Gorman, vice president of the Insurance Information Institute and Insurance Trade Association, told \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.edmunds.com\/car-safety\/traffic-ticket-urban-legends.html\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003EEdmunds\u003C\/a\u003E. \u0026quot;There are no major insurance companies that consider car color when determining your rates.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EThat\u2019s probably the best evidence that this is an urban myth\u2014if red cars really affected drivers\u0027 habits, insurance companies would certainly charge more to insure them.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ESo, what factors actually influence traffic ticket rates? Certain car models seem more likely to be ticketed (but as we\u2019ll discuss in a moment, correlation is not causation).\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EIn 2010, Quality Planning, an analytics company that validates auto insurance policyholders\u0027 information, \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.forbes.com\/2010\/10\/13\/cars-that-get-ticketed-most-police-speeding-lifestyle-vehicles-violations_slide.html#7715683077e4\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Ecounted traffic violations\u003C\/a\u003E for different vehicles over a one-year period. Drivers who owned a Mercedes-Benz SL-Class were four times more likely to get a violation than average.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EInterestingly, the Toyota Camry came in second; Camry drivers were about 3.5 times more likely to get a violation. The rest of the vehicles toward the top of the list tended to be relatively expensive or powerful.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EGranted, that\u2019s a fairly old analysis. In 2019, insurance comparison website \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/insurify.com\/insights\/car-models-with-the-most-speeding-tickets\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003EInsurify\u003C\/a\u003E dug into its database to provide its own count of traffic ticketability. Per those numbers, drivers who owned a Subaru WRX were the most likely to have a speeding ticket on their records, followed closely by Scion FR-S owners and Volkswagen GTI owners.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EOf course, these are one website\u2019s numbers, so they\u2019re not a representative sample of the entire United States driving population. Still, there were some interesting takeaways; all of the cars in the top ten had a base manufacturer suggested retail price below the average selling price of a car in June 2019, which Insurify listed as $37,285.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ERemember, correlation isn\u2019t causation. Even if Quality Planning\u2019s numbers are correct and expensive, powerful cars are pulled over more frequently, it\u2019s not proven whether that\u2019s a result of the cops\u2019 bias or those drivers being more prone to speeding.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EUltimately, it\u2019s important to remember that traffic cops are fairly active, and if you want to avoid a ticket, you need to follow the law. In a 2011 survey from the National Highway Traffic Safety Administration, about \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.nhtsa.gov\/sites\/nhtsa.dot.gov\/files\/2011_n_survey_of_speeding_attitudes_and_behaviors_811865.pdf\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003E9 percent\u003C\/a\u003E of drivers said that they\u2019d been pulled over for a traffic violation within the last 12 months. If that happens to you, here\u2019s some \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.answers.com\/Q\/Whats_the_best_way_to_get_out_of_a_traffic_ticket\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Egood information to keep in mind\u003C\/a\u003E to limit your chances of a costly violation.\u003C\/p\u003E","created_at":"2019-10-03 15:24:28","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-10-03 15:24:45","vote_count":{"vote_count":1},"user":{"name":"Kevin Stringer","avatar":null,"id":12,"tagline":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - Training prepares a person to be qualified for a job. Many people of different occupations continuously train to maintain, update and upgrade their skills. ","flag":0,"created_at":"2005-05-20 07:43:38","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-08-29 15:37:40","pivot":{"question_id":409265504,"topic_id":2147,"id":100493220}},{"id":6362,"slug":"us-military-boot-camp","title":"US Military Boot Camp","description":"Officially called the Basic Combat Training, the boot camp is a ten-week training course that introduces a civilian to teamwork, marksmanship and the basic training to become a soldier. It\u2019s open to anyone of either gender who is mentally and physically fit.","flag":0,"created_at":"2009-05-24 01:38:20","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-08-29 15:39:51","pivot":{"question_id":409265504,"topic_id":6362,"id":100493221}}],"answers":[{"id":36491876,"question_id":409265504,"user_id":12,"body":"\u003Cp\u003EAlso known as \u0026quot;boot camp,\u0026quot; basic training teaches the traditions and tactics necessary for military service. In films and television, basic training is often shown as extremely difficult and physically demanding; however, the actual experience differs significantly from portrayals in movies like \u003Cem\u003EFull Metal Jacket\u003C\/em\u003E.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EWhile the physical components of basic training plans can be difficult, the goal of most camps is to get the recruit into the proper mindset for military service. This means teaching them to work as a unit, follow orders, and put the needs of their country, unit, and military branch ahead of their individual needs.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EDrill sergeants do sometimes yell at recruits\u2014and some of them yell a lot\u2014but they tend to address groups rather than individuals. They are not allowed to physically touch recruits, and their gruff behavior always has a specific intent behind it.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;I think the biggest misconception about drill sergeants is that we are paid (to be) mean\u2014which completely isn\u0027t true,\u0026quot; said Staff Sergeant Stephanie Rodriguez, a drill sergeant from Fort Jackson, South Carolina, in a piece on the United States Army\u2019s \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.army.mil\/article\/19408\/drill_sergeants_debunk_myths\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Ewebsite\u003C\/a\u003E. \u0026quot;It\u0027s more of a tough love-type story. There is a time for discipline and a time for praise, and where I am from, everyone gets both.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EDuring boot camp, recruits are \u003Ca href=\u0022http:\/\/www.militaryspot.com\/resources\/can-i-communicate-with-my-spouse-during-basic-training\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Egenerally unable\u003C\/a\u003E to contact civilians, except via handwritten letters and extremely limited phone time. This is to compel recruits to focus completely on their training.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EDifferent branches of the United States military have different basic training camps, and the exact experience of boot camp varies for individual military personnel. Here\u2019s an overview:\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ENavy\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EIn 2018, Navy leaders increased boot camp difficulty, focusing on the \u0026quot;gritty fundamentals of physical fitness, standing watch, and waging war at sea,\u0026quot: \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.navytimes.com\/news\/your-navy\/2018\/11\/24\/not-your-daddys-boot-camp-why-great-lakes-got-tougher-2\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Eper\u003C\/a\u003E \u003Cem\u003ENavy Times\u003C\/em\u003E. Trainers cut down on computer-based training and set a new, loftier physical fitness standard. Since implementing the changes, \u0026quot;2 percent more recruits were shown the door.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;I\u2019m okay with that,\u0026quot; Rear Admiral Mike Bernacchi told Navy Times. \u0026quot;Not everybody can be a sailor.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;If it doesn\u2019t have to do with firefighting, damage control, seamanship, force protection or watch standing, we flushed it,\u0026quot; he said of the changes to the program. \u0026quot;We flushed over two weeks of curriculum.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EWhile physical training makes up a significant part of Navy boot camp, recruits also learn essential skills to protect their eventual vessels. If they fail at their tasks, they\u2019re fined real money. Recruit division commanders will also wake recruits in the middle of the night to test whether they\u2019re able to respond to emergency situations (for instance, a man overboard).\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EAt the end of training, recruits visit the USS Trayer, a building with a mock-up of an Arleigh Burke-class destroyer. Recruits must pass overnight exercise requirements in order to graduate.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ELength of boot camp:\u003C\/strong\u003E Seven weeks (plus one difficult, but unofficial, processing week)\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EArmy\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ELike the Navy, the Army increased graduation requirements for basic training in 2018. The new philosophy: Test soldiers under pressure. The focus shifted from learning concepts just once in a classroom to applying that knowledge in the field.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;You\u2019ll touch a training event such as first aid, and you\u2019ll continue to see that,\u0026quot; Command Sergeant Major Lamont Christian told \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.armytimes.com\/news\/your-army\/2018\/04\/08\/the-army-just-made-it-harder-to-graduate-from-basic-training\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003E\u003Cem\u003EArmy Times\u003C\/em\u003E\u003C\/a\u003E. \u0026quot;You\u2019ll see it all day the next day, you\u2019ll see it again the following day\u2014even though you\u2019re on the range.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ERecruits learn shooting, radio communications, Army history, grooming standards, proper dress, the \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.goarmy.com\/soldier-life\/being-a-soldier\/living-the-army-values.html\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003ESeven Core Army Values\u003C\/a\u003E, and the U.S. Soldier\u2019s Creed. Aspiring soldiers may have to perform difficult tasks while sleep deprived.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ELength of boot camp:\u003C\/strong\u003E 10 weeks\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EMarine Corps\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ECompared to other basic training programs, Marine boot camp is intense.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;Generally, no one argues that Marines\u2019 boot camp is, by far, the hardest of U.S. military indoctrinatory exercises and is famous\/infamous for its training tactics,\u0026quot; \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/slate.com\/human-interest\/2013\/03\/why-is-boot-camp-so-intense.html\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Ewrote\u003C\/a\u003E Marine sergeant Jon Davis.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ERecruits \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.military.com\/join-armed-forces\/marine-corps-boot-camp-schedule.html\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Elearn\u003C\/a\u003E basic marksmanship, military history, first aid, combat water survival, proper dress, Marine Corps Core Values, and other essentials. They must master an 11-station obstacle course, which requires significant upper body strength.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;Physical training takes many forms, but generally centers on building instant obedience to orders over actual exercise,\u0026quot; Davis wrote. \u0026quot;Most of the time, it centers on listen and do what you are told, get through the exercise, and get out of the situation before you are yelled at.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EAt the end of their training, recruits participate in the Crucible, a 54-hour exercise filled with tough physical activities. Recruits get about eight hours of sleep during the entire exercise and walk over 40 miles.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;Some [recruits] come from middle-class homes where everything has been handed to them,\u0026quot; drill sergeant Roger Summers told Military.com. \u0026quot;Others come from poorer homes where nothing was ever expected of them. If they finish the Crucible, they have accomplished something.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ELength of boot camp:\u003C\/strong\u003E \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.mcrdpi.marines.mil\/Recruit-Training\/Training-Summary-By-Week\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003E12 weeks\u003C\/a\u003E (plus four days of processing)\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EAir Force\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EThe basic training program for the Air Force \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.military.com\/join-armed-forces\/air-force-bmt-boot-camp-schedule.html\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Ebegins\u003C\/a\u003E in San Antonio, Texas. Air Force boot camp has a reputation for emphasizing history, the honor code, and self-discipline\u2014not so much on physical training.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EIn recent years, that has changed somewhat. In 2018, the Air Force added another week to basic training, and there\u2019s now more physical fitness training (44 sessions, as opposed to 31 prior to the changes).\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;There are a lot of health benefits that go along with being physically fit,\u0026quot; Master Sergeant Robert Kaufman \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.airforcetimes.com\/news\/your-air-force\/2018\/11\/05\/the-new-bmt-the-air-force-has-added-another-week-to-basic-training\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Etold\u003C\/a\u003E \u003Cem\u003EAir Force Times\u003C\/em\u003E. \u0026quot;We want them to go to their first base ready to roll, and pass that fitness test, and make that a lifestyle.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EThe new program also includes more weapons training and classes on Air Force heroes. Recruits \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.military.com\/military-fitness\/air-force-fitness-requirements\/air-force-basic-military-training-fitness-test\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Emust pass\u003C\/a\u003E the Basic Military Physical Fitness Test, which includes one minute of push-ups, one minute of sit-ups, and a timed 1.5-mile run.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ELength of boot camp:\u003C\/strong\u003E 8.5 weeks\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ECoast Guard\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EAlthough the \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.gocoastguard.com\/active-duty-careers\/enlisted-opportunities\/advance-through-training\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003ECoast Guard\u003C\/a\u003E is the smallest of the services, its basic training is nothing to sneeze at. In addition to physical requirements like being able to complete a swim circuit, Coast Guard recruits learn about military justice, customs, ethics, and Coast Guard history at their basic training in Cape May, New Jersey.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ELength of boot camp:\u003C\/strong\u003E Eight weeks\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EAgain, these are overviews\u2014no two recruits have identical experiences, and some programs (for instance, the Navy SEALs) have far different requirements than what we\u2019ve outlined above. Most basic training programs have similar goals: To form recruits into military professionals. That\u2019s typically a difficult process from the recruit\u2019s perspective, but not insurmountable.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;Normal people can\u2019t do the things warriors are asked to do,\u0026quot; Davis wrote. \u0026quot;They can\u2019t imagine it and shouldn\u2019t be forced to. But there are those that do. Both were massive hits\u2014weirdness is in the eye of the beholder.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EWith that said, the two main comics companies, Marvel and DC Comics, have had plenty of oddball outliers over their combined 165 years of business, and some are inarguably...well, weird. Here are a few of our favorites, in no particular order.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cul\u003E\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EArm-Fall-Off-Boy\u003C\/strong\u003E - \u003Cem\u003EDC Comics\u003C\/em\u003E - Contrary to his name, Arm-Fall-Off-Boy\u2019s arms didn\u2019t fall off on their own\u2014he\u2019d pull one off, then use it to hit his enemies.\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EHindsight Lad\u003C\/strong\u003E - \u003Cem\u003EMarvel\u003C\/em\u003E - He\u2019s able to analyze how events might have played out if things had gone differently\u2014so, basically, he chides much better superheroes about their performance. Hindsight Lad (later renamed Hindsight) has no superpowers.\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EHellcow\u003C\/strong\u003E - \u003Cem\u003EMarvel\u003C\/em\u003E - A cow turned into a vampire by Dracula. Hellcow produces \u201cvampiric milk\u0022 and has super-bovine strength.\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EWhizzer\u003C\/strong\u003E - \u003Cem\u003EMarvel\u003C\/em\u003E - An unfortunately named hero who received his powers after receiving a transfusion of mongoose blood. Mongooses are fast, but not exceptionally fast compared to other animals\u2014Whizzer benefited from the transfusion because of a bizarre genetic mutation.\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ECombo Man\u003C\/strong\u003E - \u003Cem\u003EMarvel\u003C\/em\u003E - A combination of at least 12 other Marvel characters, including Punisher, The Hulk, Magneto, Captain America, and the Human Torch (among others). He activated his powers by eating Combos, a popular pretzel snack. Combo Man was created as part of a promotional agreement between Marvel and Eagle Snacks, the company that produces Combos.\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EBulletman\u003C\/strong\u003E - \u003Cem\u003EFawcett Comics, and later, DC Comics\u003C\/em\u003E - A ballistics officer who developed a bullet-shaped helmet that can deflect bullets. He could fly and had superhuman strength, but mainly defeated his opponents by punching them (he didn\u2019t carry a weapon). He was sometimes accompanied by his wife, Bulletgirl. Bulletman was an extremely popular character for Fawcett Comics.\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EThe Red Bee\u003C\/strong\u003E - \u003Cem\u003EQuality Comics, and later, DC Comics\u003C\/em\u003E - An attorney who fought gangsters with trained bees. He had no superpowers, but he wore a striped suit with puffy sleeves. His favorite bee, Michael, lived in his belt buckle.\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EBouncing Boy\u003C\/strong\u003E - \u003Cem\u003EDC Comics\u003C\/em\u003E - After accidentally drinking a super-plastic formula (he mistook it for a soda), Bouncing Boy gained the ability to inflate his body to look like a giant ball. He can bounce great distances, and is somewhat impervious to damage while in ball form.\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EUlysses Solomon Archer\u003C\/strong\u003E - \u003Cem\u003EMarvel\u003C\/em\u003E - A trucker who gained the ability to pick up CB radio frequencies through his skull after receiving a partial skull replacement with an experimental alloy. He can also give lethal headbutts, and he can cycle through CB stations by using his tongue to touch the fillings in his teeth.\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EFrog-Man\u003C\/strong\u003E - \u003Cem\u003EMarvel\u003C\/em\u003E - An occasional Spider-Man sidekick, Frog-Man has an electrically powered frog suit capable of jumping great distances at a time. However, the suit\u2019s springs make navigation difficult, and Frog-Man frequently lands off course.\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003E3-D Man\u003C\/strong\u003E - \u003Cem\u003EMarvel\u003C\/em\u003E - This one\u2019s complicated: Test pilot Chuck Chandler had a run in with some aliens that turned him into an image imprinted on a pair of eyeglasses. When his brother, Hal, wears the glasses, he can cause a dimensional shift that allows the image to become flesh-and-blood 3-D Man, and the brothers share consciousness inside 3-D Man\u2019s body. 3-D Man has superhuman strength, speed, and stamina.\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ELitterbug\u003C\/strong\u003E - \u003Cem\u003EMarvel\u003C\/em\u003E - A soldier who is transformed into a giant cockroach-like creature capable of burrowing underground. Here you can ask questions about music and the hottest songs of today and yesterday.","flag":0,"created_at":"2013-01-29 18:27:53","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-08-29 15:37:31","pivot":{"question_id":409265488,"topic_id":1873,"id":100493164}},{"id":8662,"slug":"smartphone-apps","title":"Smartphone Apps","description":"Smartphone applications are software programs downloaded onto smartphones like the iPhone, Droid or BlackBerry for specific tasks. ","flag":0,"created_at":"2010-12-02 17:51:19","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-08-29 15:41:06","pivot":{"question_id":409265488,"topic_id":8662,"id":100493165}},{"id":7357,"slug":"spotify","title":"Spotify","description":"\u0022Spotify\u0022 is a FREE music program, giving you access to thousands of tracks from the comfort of your computer. The Premium version of Spotify can also be accessed on your iPhone, iPod Touch, Android and most other hand-held devices to give you access to music wherever you are!","flag":0,"created_at":"2013-06-07 16:08:52","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-08-29 15:40:22","pivot":{"question_id":409265488,"topic_id":7357,"id":100493166}},{"id":23204,"slug":"Music_Streaming_Services","title":"Music Streaming Services","description":null,"flag":0,"created_at":"2019-09-30 15:41:13","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-09-30 15:41:21","pivot":{"question_id":409265488,"topic_id":23204,"id":100493167}}],"answers":[{"id":36491863,"question_id":409265488,"user_id":12,"body":"\u003Cp\u003EThere\u2019s no simple answer, as Spotify has a fairly complicated payment structure, but by delving into the site\u2019s terms of service and looking at a few recent stats, we can get a good idea of how much (or how little) artists make.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EFirst, it\u2019s important to recognize that a paid play (i.e., a play from a Spotify Premium user) \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/artists.spotify.com\/videos\/the-game-plan\/premium-vs-free\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Epays differently\u003C\/a\u003E than a free play. When a Spotify user listens to a song with the free version of the service, the copyright holder receives a payment from the site\u2019s total generated ad revenue. When a user listens to a song on Spotify Premium, the copyright holder receives part of the site\u2019s total subscription income.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EAs ad revenue, Spotify Premium subscription counts, and total play counts change, so do the payouts. Average artist payments change significantly from year to year.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EIn December 2018, \u003Cem\u003EDigital Music News\u003C\/em\u003E \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.digitalmusicnews.com\/2018\/12\/25\/streaming-music-services-pay-2019\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Epublished\u003C\/a\u003E a list of average streaming royalty payouts from various streaming services. Spotify ranked toward the bottom of the list with an average payout of $0.00437 per play (that\u2019s about 4 tenths of a cent). The publication estimated that a musician would need about 336,842 listens per month to make $1,472 (the federal minimum wage in the United States).\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EAnother crucial note: Digital streaming payments go to the copyright holder, not straight to the artist. If an artist is on a label, their payments might be split among producers, songwriters, distributors, sampled artists, and anyone else who had a hand in the creation and promotion of the song. In other words, the artist might get a small fraction of that $0.00437.\u003C\/p.\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EHere\u2019s how the other major streaming services compare, based on the late-2018 numbers:\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cul\u003E\u003Cli\u003ENapster - $0.019 per play\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003ETIDAL - $0.0125 per play\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EApple Music - $0.00735 per play\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EGoogle Play Music - $0.00676 per play\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EDeezer - $0.0064 per play\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003ESpotify - $0.00437 per play\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EAmazon - $0.00402 per play\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EPandora - $0.00133 per play\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EYouTube - $0.00069 per play\u003C\/li\u003E\u003C\/ul\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003ESo, why are streaming sites so stingy? For one, they don\u2019t make much money\u2014or, in some cases, any money. The only time Spotify has turned a profit in its 13 years of business was the \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.theverge.com\/2019\/2\/6\/18214331\/spotify-earnings-financial-announcement-profits-music-streaming-podcast\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Elast quarter\u003C\/a\u003E of 2018. At this point, music subscription services haven\u2019t quite figured out how to make significant revenue from their user bases.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EEvery major streaming service changes its payouts regularly, so take the numbers above with a grain of salt. The takeaway is that musicians don\u2019t make much, but streaming services don\u2019t, either.\u003C\/p\u003E","created_at":"2019-09-30 15:47:09","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-09-30 15:47:09","vote_count":{"vote_count":2},"user":{"name":"Kevin Stringer","avatar":null,"id":12,"tagline":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - James Brown","meta":[{"user_id":12,"key":"tagline","value":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - James Brown"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optInEmail","value":"0"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optInNotifications","value":"1"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optOutAll","value":"0"}]}}],"vote_count":{"id":57820514,"vote_count":2,"votable_id":409265488,"votable_type":"App\\Models\\Question","created_at":"2019-09-30 15:54:23","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-10-09 06:54:21"},"meta":[{"id":13,"question_id":409265488,"key":"is_locked","value":"false","created_at":"2019-10-15 20:33:01","updated_at":"2019-10-15 20:41:56"}],"user":{"id":12,"username":"stringer","name":"Kevin Stringer","tagline":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - James Brown","meta":[{"user_id":12,"key":"tagline","value":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - James Brown"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optInEmail","value":"0"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optInNotifications","value":"1"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optOutAll","value":"0"}]}},{"id":409265480,"user_id":5,"slug":"What_are_some_odd_jobs_that_pay_surprisingly_well","title":"What are some odd jobs that pay surprisingly well","body":null,"created_at":"2019-09-30 14:07:20","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-10-02 21:09:21","link":"https:\/\/www.answers.com\/Q\/What_are_some_odd_jobs_that_pay_surprisingly_well","topics":[{"id":1868,"slug":"jobs","title":"Jobs","description":"Workers of the world unite! Similarly, every time that team\u2019s closest competitor for the division (or Wild Card) loses, the magic number also decreases by one.\u0026quot; Basically, the magic number is used to measure how close a team is to clinching a spot in the playoffs.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EMLB.com goes on to discuss the first ever usage of the statistic during pennant race of the 1947 season. \u0026quot;An article in the Sept. 12, 1947 edition of the Washington Post stated: %27The Yankees reduced the magic number to four. Team A, who is currently in the lead, has a record of 87-65, while Team B is just behind them with a record of 85-67.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EThe formula for determining Team A\u2019s magic number is the following:\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003E\u003Cem\u003EM = G + 1 - What happens if this is more than I can afford?\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EHow does your debt repayment plan work? How will I know my creditors have received payments? Can I get access to my accounts online or by phone?\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003ECan you get my creditors to lower or eliminate interest and finance charges or waive late fees?\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EIs a debt repayment plan my only option?\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EWhat if I can\u0027t maintain the agreed-upon plan?\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EWhat debts will be excluded from the debt repayment plan?\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EWill you help me plan for payment of these debts?\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EWho will help me if I have problems with my accounts or creditors?\u003C\/li\u003E\n\u003Cli\u003EHow secure is the information I provide to you?\u003C\/li\u003E\u003C\/ul\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EYou might wish to check with your state Attorney General, local consumer protection agency, and the Better Business Bureau to find out if consumers have filed complaints about the provider you are considering. Any reputable credit counseling agency should send you free information about itself and the services it provides without requiring you to provide any details about your situation. If not, consider that a red flag and go elsewhere for help.\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EBe careful before choosing Consumer Credit Counseling. Research this option using the internet and verify everything. CCC companies have gotten a very bad name in recent years based on changes in their industry. Credit counseling was originally started by the credit card companies themselves as an alternative means for collecting their debt from consumers. These companies typically never reduce the amount owed to their participants, sometimes renegotiating late fees and over-the-limit charges. This is the \u0026quot;savings\u0026quot; that they advertise. \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.nationaldebtrelief.com\/the-ugly-truth-about-credit-counseling\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003EAccording to\u003C\/a\u003E NationalDebtRelief, \u0026quot;the success rate of debt management plans is about 20% to 26%.\u0026quot; They also go on to recommend considering \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.nationaldebtrelief.com\/debt-consolidation\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Edebt consolidation loans\u003C\/a\u003E and Chapter 7 bankruptcies, citing their \u0026quot;better completion rates.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\n\u003Cp\u003EIt is also important to note that when filing for bankruptcy, whether Chapter 7 or Chapter 13, that you are \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.nolo.com\/legal-encyclopedia\/the-pre-bankruptcy-credit-counseling-requirement.html\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Erequired to provide\u003C\/a\u003E a certificate showing that you received credit counseling from an agency approved by the U.S. Trustee\u0027s office within the 180-day period before you file for bankruptcy.\u003C\/p\u003E","created_at":"2015-07-17 17:30:21","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-10-02 19:39:10","vote_count":{"vote_count":2},"user":{"name":"Kevin Stringer","avatar":null,"id":12,"tagline":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - ","flag":0,"created_at":"2010-12-05 11:40:15","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-08-29 15:37:02","pivot":{"question_id":409265447,"topic_id":470,"id":100493046}}],"answers":[{"id":36491787,"question_id":409265447,"user_id":12,"body":"\u003Cp\u003EThe only safe way to avoid a traffic ticket is to avoid breaking the law in the first place, but if you\u2019re asking this question, you\u2019re past that point. In the United States, \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.valuepenguin.com\/2019\/07\/speeding-leading-cause-fatal-car-crashes-us\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Ean average\u003C\/a\u003E of 125,000 people receive speeding tickets each day. A single speeding offense can increase a driver\u2019s insurance premiums by 25 percent, depending on the nature of the offense and other factors, so it\u2019s no wonder that drivers look for ways to avoid tickets.\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003EThe bad news: The police officer who pulls you over will ultimately decide whether or not to give you the ticket, and there\u2019s no magic phrase that will work 100 percent of the time. Still, there are a few key ways to minimize your risks:\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ERemain friendly and considerate throughout the interaction.\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003ESmile, answer the officer\u2019s questions, and don\u2019t try to butt in with explanations for your behavior. Even if you feel that the police are being rude, don\u2019t respond in kind.\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;Never tell a police officer that \u2018I pay your salary\u2019 because, well, so do they, because they pay taxes too,\u0026quot; traffic ticket attorney Jay Ruane told Urbo.\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003EA former NYPD officer, who asked to remain anonymous in the Urbo piece, agreed with that assessment.\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003E\u0026quot;Don\u2019t huff and puff, roll your eyes, or imply for them to hurry up because you have more important things to do,\u0026quot; he said. \u0026quot;Most cops don\u2019t like ruining your day, but we get paid to try to make the roadways a bit safer for our families and everyone else\u2019s. Don\u2019t minimize that.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EDon\u2019t admit guilt.\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003EIf you don\u2019t think you did anything wrong, resist the urge to apologize. Remember, if you get a ticket, you may be able to fight it in court, but admitting guilt will seal your fate before you get a chance to speak to a judge. If the police ask if you know why you were pulled over, you can always answer with, \u0026quot;No, sir.\u0026quot;\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003EBy the same token, you shouldn\u2019t lie. Don\u2019t make up excuses or insist that the officer\u2019s wrong. Stay calm and collected. When in doubt, say nothing and allow the officer to make their case.\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EShow consideration for the officer\u2019s safety.\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003EPolice constantly deal with dangerous situations. Pull over as soon as the officer signals for you to do so, and where possible, pull far enough off the road that the officer will be able to easily access your car without standing near oncoming traffic.\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003EKeep your hands on the steering wheel. If you need to get your license, insurance cards, or other documents, ask the officer before reaching for them. Follow all of the officer\u2019s instructions to the best of your ability.\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EAsk for a warning.\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003EContrary to popular belief, most police departments say they don\u2019t have traffic ticket quotas, and some cities and states \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.illinoispolicy.org\/rauner-signs-bill-banning-ticket-quotas-in-chicago\/\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Ehave prohibitions\u003C\/a\u003E on such quotas. Generally speaking, the officer will decide whether you get your ticket, so if you don\u2019t want one, ask for a warning.\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003ERemain polite and respectful while making your request. If you have a reasonable explanation for your behavior\u2014for instance, it\u2019s the end of a long day, your kid\u2019s hungry, and you accidentally rolled a stop sign\u2014feel free to share, but don\u2019t tell a long, drawn-out story. If your offense is minor, asking for a warning might work. Then again, it might not, but what\u2019s the harm in asking?\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Ch2\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ENever try to bribe or intimidate the officer.\u003C\/strong\u003E\u003C\/h2\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003EThat\u2019s a quick way to turn a minor traffic ticket into a much more serious charge. In New York, for instance, \u003Ca href=\u0022https:\/\/www.new-york-lawyers.org\/bribery-in-the-third-degree-new-york-penal-law-200-00.html\u0022 rel=\u0022nofollow ugc\u0022\u003Ebribery carries\u003C\/a\u003E a possible penalty of up to seven years in state prison.\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003EIf you get a traffic ticket, you still have options. You can fight the ticket by appearing in court, and in some circumstances, you can request court supervision instead of a conviction (meaning if you abide by your sentence, the blemish on your record won\u2019t be as serious).\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\u003Cp\u003EJust remember: During a traffic stop, your goal is to be civil, reasonable, and conscious of the officer\u2019s safety. Whether you\u2019re avoiding a traffic ticket or trying to fight it in court, a polite attitude goes a long way.\u003C\/p\u003E\r\n\r\n\u003Cp\u003EOriginally posted on Urbo.com\u003C\/p\u003E","created_at":"2019-09-24 20:12:25","deleted_at":null,"updated_at":"2019-09-26 15:24:47","vote_count":{"vote_count":3},"user":{"name":"Kevin Stringer","avatar":null,"id":12,"tagline":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - James Brown","meta":[{"user_id":12,"key":"tagline","value":"\u0022I am real.\u0022 - James Brown"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optInEmail","value":"0"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optInNotifications","value":"1"},{"user_id":12,"key":"optOutAll","value":"0"}]}},{"id":36493383,"question_id":409265447,"user_id":26534,"body":"Kami menawarkan Jasa Seo yang mampu mendorong website Anda ke halaman 1 Google.
